Chapter 1401: That Was Amazingly Splendid. (1)

“Enemy ahead! Enemy, soldiers!”

“It is Mount Hua!”

“Mount Hua is attacking, Commander!”

A frantic, no, a desperate wail poured out.

But that cry did not reach Ho Ga-Myung. Frozen like a stone in place, he stared with wide eyes at the onrushing figures.

‘Why?’

Ho Ga-Myung, faced with something beyond the limits of his imagination, could think nothing, could make no response.

‘How?’

Why is the Heavenly Friends Alliance, the Mount Hua, here?

It shouldn’t have been possible to find them. Even if they had crossed the Yangtze, in this vast Southern land it would have been impossible to track their whereabouts. So how did this happen?

“Why…”

A stifled voice slipped out between his lips. What had been a mutter swelled and finally burst into a shout.

“Whaaaaat forrrrr!”

The whole forest echoed.

Footsteps shook the earth, and with that rumble a hundred Mount Hua disciples charged as one. The rage in their eyes poured out directly onto the Ten Thousand People Clan members who threatened Mount Hua’s swords.

No one had given orders. Nothing had been prepared in advance.

Yet everyone’s will to run toward a single place naturally formed their ranks into a single shape.

A wedge piercing the enemy. No, closer to a straight, thrusting blade, Mount Hua’s swordsmen formed a formation like a long spear and, with explosive force, drove toward the Ten Thousand People Clan’s encirclement.

“Oh, they’re coming!”

“B-b-block them… No! Get out of the way! Move! Move!”

Urgent screams burst out everywhere. But they soon had to face one-armed swordsmen who, like asura, let out a killing intent and charged.

“Oooooo!”

The Plum Blossom Sword, filled with red sword qi, carried boiling rage and struck the enemy’s neck.

Swoooosh!

A Ten Thousand People Clan disciple’s head, cut off with a face twisted in terror, shot into the air.

Having severed a life in an instant, Un Geom leapt into the enemy lines at an even greater speed.

“Break in!”

Kraaaang!

Hyun Sang slammed his foot into the ground and spread his sword. Red sword qi bloomed and spread like plum blossoms in all directions.

“R-retreat… Gyaaah!”

Ten Thousand People Clan disciples were helplessly pierced by the sword energy.

The two swords at the front were strong. And the swords of the Mount Hua disciples that followed were even more powerful.

Swoooosh!

Red sword qi kept bursting like fireworks. From above, it would look like red plum blossoms blooming in sequence along the path of the vanguard.

Truly a beautiful, fantastical sight.

But the beauty concealed a deadly blade.

Shick! Shick!

Each time the sword qi, fluttering like petals and seeming infinitely delicate, scattered in all directions, crimson wounds inevitably bloomed on the enemies’ bodies.

“Aaaaaaargh!”

A cold, blue killing intent rippled in the eyes of Mount Hua’s swordsmen.

Because they could see clearly what was before them: their sahyung, wounded all over the body and dying.

Crunch!

No one shouted insults at the enemy. No one cheered those around them. Instead, they poured their overflowing anger and hatred into their blades without reserve.

Even shouting was a luxury. There was only one thing they had to do now: save their bleeding sahyung-sajaes.

Swoooosh!

The honed red sword energy mercilessly ravaged the enemy. Mount Hua’s swordsmen, united as a single red blade, moved forward, only forward.

“D-damn, stop them! Don’t let them break through…”

Krrraak!

The one who had tried to shout in panic fell backward. A black poisoned dagger stuck in the mouth of the one who died without even closing his eyes.

“P-poison!”

“It’s the Tang Family! Aah! Bow your heads!”

Swoooosh!

They tried to reorganize their ranks somehow, but countless daggers flew from above and black poison dispersed, so there was nothing they could do. The Ten Thousand People Clan’s formation began to crumble.

“Provide cover! Don’t let Mount Hua gain a foothold over us!”

At Tang Zhan’s shout, the Tang Family members screamed at the top of their lungs.

In front, Mount Hua’s disciples fired like a single blade, while overhead the Tang Family launched hidden weapons and poison that scattered.

It was a truly vicious combination. Even a Ten Thousand People Clan at full strength would find it difficult to face this, not to mention the Ten Thousand People Clan who had nearly exhausted themselves pursuing from the Southern Island to here.

“Why, why are Mount Hua’s bastards here!”

“D-don’t push, damn you!”

Those trying to retreat, those desperately trying to fight, and the hesitant all tangled together as the formation fell apart.

Around the red-blooming Plum Blossoms, green flashes of light embroidered the air.

At that moment, a Ten Thousand People Clan disciple shouted.

“Damn it! Don’t panic, there aren’t that many of them…!”

Kraaaang!

But his body was swept away by a massive white sword qi that came from somewhere. Following it, swordsmen dressed in sky-blue robes appeared.

“Save the Young lord and the alliance members!”

“Acknowledged!”

A blue sky was cast over the Plum Blossom grove. The Namgung Family’s swordsmen, fitting the name of blue heavens perfectly, rushed with all their might toward the Ten Thousand People Clan disciples.

“Palace Lord! We’ve arrived!”

Following them came swordsmen in ice-cold white robes, and warriors of the Beast Palace led by Beast Palace Lord Meng So also charged in.

“Sweep away the Ten Thousand People Clan bastard!”

“Yes!”

With Meng So’s roar booming as if to echo to the heavens, the Beast Palace forces charged fiercely like predators hunting prey.

Those who called each other friends. Those who called themselves the alliance members had gathered here to protect those they must protect.

And then.

“Save Mount Hua’s Sword Saint! Now is the time to repay at least a bit of debt!”

“Yes!”

With Southern Island Sect Leader Geum Yang-Baek’s cry, the Southern Island’s disciples led by Gwak Hwan-So also charged with all their strength.

Though their bodies were already exhausted, those who spoke of chivalry with their mouths and of justice with their swords had duties they must perform even if it cost them their last breath.

United in one will, they surged toward the enemy like lightning.

“Damn it! Why are they here…!”

“Block them! Blo…”

Swords flew continuously toward the enemies who slashed like madmen.

Swoooosh!

A severed neck shot into the air. Hyun Jong gripped the Plum Blossom Sword tightly, having cut an enemy’s head off with a single, merciless strike.

His children were right there.

Bleeding all over their bodies.

Seeing disciples too weak to raise their bodies, a fierce anger unlike anything he’d ever felt before rose in Hyun Jong’s chest.

Who did this?

Who dared make those children bleed?

Swoooosh!

A sword steeped in immense internal qi cleaved a retreating Ten Thousand People Clan member in two at once. Warm blood splashed and stained the white hair, but Hyun Jong walked the blood-soaked path without hesitation.

This was the price for his hesitation.

The blood he should have shed was being shed by the children.

Really though, who are they? Who made those children bleed?

Kraaaaang!

The Purple Cloud Divine Arts unleashed at full intensity.

A tremendous sword qi fell forward, causing an explosion so massive it was hard to even stand.

Shick!

At that moment a blade flew from the flank and brushed Hyun Jong’s cheek. Skin split and blood spattered, but Hyun Jong felt not even the smallest pain.

What truly pained him was not wounds carved into this old body.

Each nick on the children’s bodies, every drop of blood spilled here, lodged into Hyun Jong’s heart like rusted daggers.

“Ugh, aaaah!”

Kraaaang!

Hyun Jong stomped the ground after cleaving an enemy in two with a single strike.

“Cover the former Sect Leader!”

Mount Hua’s swordsmen moved in perfect unison to Hyun Jong’s left and right.

Their will was communicated without words. No order was needed; everyone looked to the same place.

“D-damn, stop…”

Krrrak! Krrrak! Krrrak! Krrrak!

Green flashes of light struck and pierced the skulls of those charging at Hyun Jong without fail.

Hyun Jong advanced through the collapsing bodies turned cold corpses. Red sword energy rose from his blade.

“Hyun Sang!”

“Yes!”

Martial Hall leader Hyun Sang became his sword and drove back the enemies.

“Un Geom!”

“Yes!”

White Plum Blossom Boarding Hall Leader, Un Geom became his shield and pushed away the attackers at once.

Hyun Jong advanced along that path—the path Mount Hua’s blades had pierced and Mount Hua’s shields had guarded. The path opened by everyone’s united will.

Kraaaang!

And Hyun Jong walked toward the path cleaved open by his own blade.

Step. Step.

Hyun Jong’s steps gradually slowed.

Step. Step.

And after a moment, his footsteps stopped completely.

Having broken through all who stood in his way to come here, Hyun Jong silently gazed at those before him.

His vision kept blurring. If he parted his lips even slightly, sobs, no, wails, would pour out.

“Ah…”

A dazed voice escaped someone’s mouth, as if they couldn’t believe the reality of Hyun Jong being here.

Step.

Hyun Jong stepped a little closer and gave a heavy pat to the shoulder his hand reached.

“Sect…”

Jo Gul stared at him as if spellbound.

“Sect le…”

Yoon Jong, Tang Soso, and Yu Yiseol as well.

Hyun Jong, touching the shoulders of disciples still unable to let go of their swords one by one, finally stood before someone kneeling on one knee at the very back.

He stood in silence and just looked for a moment, at Baek Cheon, clutching Chung Myung as if protecting him even in this state.

They say the chest feels torn; this must be what they mean.

Just as Hyun Jong reached out with a trembling hand, Baek Cheon flinched and moved. Hyun Jong’s hand stopped abruptly.

“I report to the Former Sect Leader…”

Even so, Baek Cheon managed to compose himself and saluted Hyun Jong.

“The Great Mount Hua Sect’s… Acting Sect Leader Baek Cheon, I could not fully… carry out the order, but…”

“…”

“…I carried the mission out without betraying Mount Hua’s will.”

Hyun Jong’s paused hand moved slowly again and then settled onto Baek Cheon’s shoulder.

A hand no longer trembling gently patted Baek Cheon’s shoulder.

“Amazing.”

“…”

“Amazingly splendid.” This time Baek Cheon’s shoulders trembled in small motions. Hyun Jong accepted that tremor as it was and nodded slowly.

A gentle smile bloomed at the corners of his wrinkled mouth. Tears gathered at his eyes and the kindly smile reassured those who saw it.

“Leave the rear to me and the sahyung-sajaes. We’re here now.”

Mount Hua’s disciples, smashing through the enemy, surrounded and escorted their wounded sahyung like bodyguards.

Seeing backs imbued with resolute will, Baek Cheon finally let go of what he’d been suppressing and suppressed again. His eyes grew hot, and tears streamed down his face already dried by blood.

“…I receive the command, Former Sect Leader.”

Just as Chung Myung was not alone, they themselves were not alone.

They were here. The place they had to go.

The place they were supposed to return to had come to them here.

Chapter 1402: That Was Amazingly Splendid. (2)

“Hold the line! Defend with your lives!”

Rough voices swept across the battlefield.

Everyone surged like a torrent, encircling the besieged Five Swords and their party. Mount Hua’s disciples, Tang Family members, and even the swordsmen from the Namgung Family who arrived afterward rushed the enemies and pushed them back.

“Fall back, you dogs of the Evil Sect!”

Into the space created by the Heavenly Friends Alliance throwing their bodies, the remaining Mount Hua disciples plunged.

“Sahyung!”

“Damn it, sasuk!”

The sight of their sahyung-sajaes in shambles stirred fierce hatred in the Mount Hua disciples’ eyes.

Un Am’s thunderous voice stopped those who, unable to contain their rage, were about to turn and charge the enemies.

“Hold your positions!”

“S-Sect leader…”

“Our task is not to hunt the enemy! Protect your sahyung-sajaes. Let none of them breach this place!”

“Yes!”

Mount Hua’s disciples, their eyes filled with killing intent, drove back the Ten Thousand People Clan members who had targeted Chung Myung and his group. The Ten Thousand People Clan, thrown by the flurry of blades, found themselves repeatedly pushed back in confusion.

Mount Hua and the Heavenly Friends Alliance formed a solid wall the enemies could not penetrate.

“Yoon Jong!”

Meanwhile, Hyun Sang, who had rushed like lightning, was momentarily at a loss for words in front of Yoon Jong. As the stunned Hyun Sang barely gathered himself to check Yoon Jong’s pulse, Yoon Jong suddenly shook his head and weakly pointed elsewhere.

“Elder… Chung Myung, take care of Chung Myung…”

“Hey, you fool! Who’s supposed to care for who right now…!”

“Elder, hurry, Chung Myung is…”

Hyun Sang clenched his lips. At that moment Baek Cheon stepped forward and handed over Chung Myung he had been holding.

“Elder…”

“…”

“My qi… Chung Myung…”

Although the words came out haltingly, Hyun Sang immediately understood the meaning.

But he could not bring himself to take Chung Myung. He had no idea where to place his hands—there wasn’t a single uninjured spot; how could he touch him?

“Stand aside.”

“S-Sect leader. Chung Myung…”

“Get a hold of yourself!”

At that moment Hyun Jong shoved Hyun Sang aside and took Chung Myung in his arms. His sleeve instantly soaked with Chung Myung’s blood, yet Hyun Jong showed no flinch as he pushed his mighty internal qi into Chung Myung’s abdomen.

“I-is he alright? Is he going to be okay?”

As Hyun Sang asked urgently, Hyun Jong hardened his face and shouted instead of answering.

“Those who have any remaining strength, tend to the wounded! Now!”

“…Yes?”

“What are you all doing! Is Chung Myung the only injured one here?”

“Yes? Ah, yes! Former Sect leader!”

Those words snapped them to attention.

Of course Chung Myung was the worst off, but the others had suffered injuries that words couldn’t fully describe.

Those with enough internal qi rushed over and began imbuing qi. Hyun Sang likewise seized Yoon Jong and poured qi into his back.

“…S-Sect leader.”

“Don’t speak, Gul!”

Un Am placed his hand on Jo Gul’s back and poured qi into him.

“I—rather than me, the others…”

“Don’t say another word!”

“I’m still fine! Start with the sasuk…”

“I told you to shut your mouth!”

Un Am bit his lip. Even now he had the mind to worry about others—whether that was kindness or stupidity, it was impossible to tell.

‘These foolish bastards…’

How could they neglect their own bodies so? He was proud, yet unbearably resentful.

Among them, there was one who obeyed the least.

“Soso! You can’t do that!”

“Let go!”

“Soso! Your injuries are severe too!”

“I’m not dead, so let go!”

Tang Soso repeatedly refused those trying to treat her and clung to Chung Myung, whom Hyun Jong was holding. Before Hyun Jong could stop her, Tang Soso grabbed Chung Myung’s wrist, checked his pulse, and lifted her head violently.

“Father? Where is the Tang Family Lord?”

“He’s holding back the enemy’s advance. He’ll be here soon.”

“Quick! As fast as possible! Former Sect leader! The sahyung is dying!”

Hyun Jong shouted as if there were no need to think.

“Un Geom! Bring the Tang Family Lord! At once!”

“That’s not necessary.”

At that moment, a streak of green light suddenly landed before them.

“Father!”

“This way.”

Tang Gunak seized Chung Myung as if snatching him away. His face, hard as rock as he checked his condition, went pale in an instant. Seeing his usually unshakeable calm crumble was more chilling than any shout or scream.

“What were you thinking!”

Tang Gunak bit his lip, laid Chung Myung out gently with hands like a carving knife, took a small box from his robe, opened it, and pulled out a dozen large golden needles.

“No one! Let no one approach us, absolutely no one!”

Hyun Jong didn’t need to say anything. The moment those words were heard, the Mount Hua disciples’ momentum shifted. Even those who could barely stand rose to protect Chung Myung, and they had to be restrained.

“Soso! Tang Pae! Help!”

“Yes!”

“Tang Family Lord. Soso also…”

“Do not interfere, step back!”

Baek Sang, who tried to stop the Tang Family Lord, paled and stepped back at the deadly command, realizing how dire the situation was.

“Tang Pae!”

“Yes, Lord!”

Tang Pae shoved aside the one pouring qi into him and leapt forward. As he attached himself to Chung Myung’s opposite side, Tang Gunak channeled qi into the extracted needles.

Wooooooom!

The golden needles in his hand trembled wildly. At the same time, Tang Gunak’s long robe swelled as if about to burst under the strain of the qi. The sheer force made even the Mount Hua disciples turn around for an instant.

As Tang Soso began to be pushed back by the qi wave, Hyun Jong grabbed her shoulder and breathed qi into her.

WOOOOOOOOOOM!

The golden needles on Tang Gunak’s palm thrashed like live carp and then quieted. The faint tremor ceased completely, and Tang Gunak thrust the needles into Chung Myung’s throat like lightning.

Chung Myung’s body convulsed on the spot. Tang Gunak pressed him down and shouted.

“Poisoned dagger, navel point, gui (face intersection) point!”

“Yes!”

Tang Pae and Tang Soso struck the pressure points Tang Gunak named.

“Three mm deeper at the navel point!”

“F-Father. Sahyung’s body…”

“Do as instructed!”

“Yes!”

Tang Soso gritted her teeth and struck the points Tang Gunak ordered. Black, dead blood gushed from Chung Myung’s mouth.

WOOOOOM!

Another large needle plunged into the center of Chung Myung’s chest. A long needle the size of an adult palm sank entirely into his body and disappeared.

“Universal Acupoint, Upper abdomen point, Foot paralysis point!”

“Yes!”

Tang Family Lord Tang Gunak was a warrior who had crossed into the realm of the absolute. Yet his face was soon soaked with sweat. The scene made clear how much spirit and power this needle technique was consuming.

Wooooooom!

Tang Gunak bit his teeth and poured power into the needles again. The sudden drain on his inner strength made his hands begin to tremble.

After a moment, the last of the twelve needles pierced into the very center of Chung Myung’s crown.

“Is it okay to do that to a person’s head?”

When someone finally couldn’t stand it and shouted, Tang Soso answered loudly.

“They’re not needles!”

“…Huh?”

She had no strength left to speak more, so she stopped answering and seized Chung Myung’s wrist, pouring qi into him.

The golden needles Tang Gunak used were not truly needles. They were spiritual items refined from rare ingredients, even the Tang Family rarely possessed them. Each one could not be valued in mere coin.

Within the Tang Family they were called the Rejuvenating Great Golden Needles. They were heirlooms permitted only to the Tang Family Lord.

In other words, Tang Gunak was, without a moment’s hesitation, expending a Tang heirloom that had been preserved for generations on someone who was not even a member of the Tang Family.

But…

“It’s not enough.”

Tang Gunak snapped his head up.

“Ice Palace! Bring the elders of the Ice Palace here! Beast Palace Lord too! Now!”

“Un Am! Hyun Sang!”

“Yes! Sect leader!”

“Open a path! Bring the Ice Palace and the Beast Palace here at once! Hurry!”

Hyun Jong and Un Am ran out with determined faces. Hyun Jong placed his hand on Chung Myung’s dantian and quietly spoke to Tang Gunak, who was channeling qi.

“…Tang Family Lord, Chung Myung…”

“I’ve done everything I can right now.”

“…Can he live?”

Tang Gunak did not answer. But Hyun Jong did not take the silence as a negative. The Tang Family Lord was pouring every ounce of remaining internal qi into tending Chung Myung—that showed his will to save him.

And Tang Gunak was not the only one showing that resolve.

“Stand aside, you vermin!”

Kraaaang!

Beast Palace Lord Meng So struck aside a dozen Ten Thousand People Clan members in one blow and arrived where they were.

“Where is the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint!”

As he bellowed loudly, Ice Palace people charged in behind him.

“Palace Lord!”

“Commander Han, not me! Over there!”

Seol So-Baek shook his head and shouted. Meng So and Han Yi-Myung immediately grasped the situation and rushed to Tang Gunak with all their might.

“Tang Family Lord!”

“Commander!”

Tang Gunak snapped his head to look at Han Yi-Myung.

“We need someone in the Ice Palace skilled the most in Yin techniques!”

“Eh? Th-that is…”

A look of embarrassment flickered across Han Yi-Myung’s face. Naturally, the one capable of the Ice Palace’s strongest Yin techniques was the Ice Palace Lord. The most powerful ice arts were cultivable only by the Ice Palace Lord.

But Seol So-Baek had not yet mastered all those ice techniques.

“It is not me, but the Palace Lord.”

“Then both of you, come!”

“Yes?”

Tang Gunak turned sharply to look at the Beast Palace Lord.

“Palace Lord! Can you use the Yang Heat techniques?”

“Th-that is the specialty of the South Sea Sun Palace, I…”

“Can you do it?”

Meng So’s face hardened.

“Is it necessary? To save Mount Hua’s Sword Saints?”

“Absolutely!”

Meng So nodded as if there were no need to think.

“Then I’ll do it! Even if I burn to death, I’ll pull it all out!”

Tang Gunak nodded and spoke quickly.

“From both sides of Mount Hua’s Sword Saints, push Yin qi and Yang qi into him. With all your strength.”

“W-what, Tang Family Lord? What do you mean?”

Meng So and Han Yi-Myung’s faces went pale in an instant.

The two qi are mutually antagonistic. When such opposing qi collide within a body, the outcome is predictably catastrophic.

They knew how to handle each qi, so they understood how dangerous this was. As the three before him hesitated, Tang Gunak shouted.

“Do as you’re told!”

But they couldn’t move easily. Tang Gunak, his face set, spoke with conviction.

“He will endure it.”

“…”

“If it’s Mount Hua’s Sword Saints, of all people, he will surely hold on!”

Meng So, Seol So-Baek, and Han Yi-Myung’s gazes turned to the fallen Chung Myung.

A wrong move and their hands might end Chung Myung’s life. So no decision came easily.

But in that moment Seol So-Baek knelt beside Chung Myung and grasped his hand.

“P-palace Lord!”

“I’ll do it!”

Seol So-Baek gritted his teeth.

“If it is this Taoist, he’ll endure! None other than Taoist Chung Myung can endure it!”

Firm belief shone in Seol So-Baek’s eyes. Seeing that, Meng So and Han Yi-Myung also nodded and took hold of Chung Myung’s hand.

“No one! Let no one approach…”

Tang Gunak, about to give final instructions, closed his mouth.

All he could see were the backs of the Mount Hua disciples surrounding them. The resolve on those backs was suffocating.

Tang Gunak drew a deep breath.

“I will definitely bring him back.”

He was holding everything of the Heavenly Friends Alliance. The one who showed what they must pursue.

And above all… he was their friend.

Chapter 1403: That Was Amazingly Splendid. (3)

A white sword qi streaked fiercely and poured into the midst of the Ten Thousand People Clan members.

KWAANG! KWAAAANG! KWAANG!

With deafening blasts that seemed to tear eardrums, the incoming sword qi detonated one after another.

“Aaaaaah!”

“Aaaaah!”

Those swept by the explosions let out agonized screams and were flung in all directions.

“Kuh, cough!”

Of course, not everyone died in the blasts. Some, by luck, survived the shockwaves.

But those groaning on the ground were met by a rain of poisonous needles that blackened the blue sky as they fell.

“Ugh, aaaaah!”

Sshhhh!

With the roar of a torrential downpour, needles thinner than hair pierced fragile skin, carrying deadly concentrations of poison.

Pt-tuk!

The thin needles pierced flesh with a tink sound. Those who felt that sound in their bodies rather than their ears were instantly gripped by unspeakable terror.

“No, don’t!”

The panicked tried desperately to pull the needles from their wounds, but the pierced areas had already blackened.

The poison of the Sichuan Tang Family—the Tang Family that had turned those four characters into a symbol of terror—was spreading through their bodies.

A faint discomfort turned into unbearable itching, which soon became searing pain that spread through them.

“Kuh… grrrr…”

The Ten Thousand People Clan members writhed on the spot. Blood flowed from their seven orifices, and black-tinged froth boiled up in their mouths.

“Get away! It’s the Tang Family! Those damned bastards use poison!”

Watching comrades blacken and die in an instant filled the eyes of the Ten Thousand People Clan members with terror.

It was incomprehensible.

How could those who used such vicious poison be called a Justice faction? Even the Evil Sects wouldn’t, couldn’t, use poison that malevolent.

“Do not panic! They’re just needles! If you block them, you’ll be fine!”

It wasn’t entirely wrong—the force carried by each needle wasn’t that great. The problem was that the people here were not only the Tang Family.

“Hiiyaaaap!”

A ripping scream—more a beast’s roar than a human shout—issued from a group that charged straight into the Ten Thousand People Clan members.

KWAAAANG!

Those hit on the shoulders were sent rolling on the ground, spraying blood.

The vanguard struck with tremendous force at those who tried to block them; others leapt over shoulders like leopards, swinging malformed weapons shaped like animal claws.

“Hey, Beast Palace!”

“These filthy barbarians!”

The members of the Southern Beast Palace, their identities proclaimed by the animal pelts draped around them, charged the Ten Thousand People Clan like enraged wild boars.

“Hahahahahaha! You Evil Sect scum! You’ve been looking down on us all this time!”

“Trample them! Completely!”

Where the Beast Palace burst forth, the Ice Palace swordsmen—emitting chilling killing intent—held the line.

“Do not break formation; stop the attackers completely. What matters is the safety of the palace lord and the survival of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint. Any foolhardy action will be met by my blade!”

“Yes!”

The raging Beast Palace and the Ice Palace swordsmen—two factions with utterly opposite natures—joined forces for a single purpose.

“C-commander!”

“We’re being pushed back! Commander! The encirclement is breaking!”

Ho Ga-Myung clenched his fist in silence.

‘Damn it.’

It was the inevitable result.

Of course, one couldn’t deny how formidable the Ten Thousand People Clan was. Among the four factions of the Four Sects Alliance that dominate the Southern land, the Ten Thousand People Clan was practically a leader-level sect.

Still, the Ten Thousand People Clan was only one sect within the Four Sects Alliance. No matter how strong a single force, it was impossible to fight alone against a coalition of the realm’s foremost sects.

Moreover, those Ho Ga-Myung led were not even the so-called ‘main force’ within the Ten Thousand People Clan.

So there was no choice but to be inexorably pushed back.

“Commander! Give an order!”

Ho Ga-Myung bit his lip.

He was right in front of him. The Plum Blossom Sword Fiend’s neck was at hand. If he had even one breath’s worth of time, he could have cut that throat!

“Why at this exact moment…”

How on earth had they found this place? He had already removed those beggar scum’s eyes long ago.

This was not in Ho Ga-Myung’s calculations. Perhaps it wasn’t even within that alliance lord’s plans.

To Ho Ga-Myung it felt as if heaven had abandoned them.

Ho Ga-Myung, who always prioritized composure, could not find calm in this moment.

“Commander! You must give some order!”

Perhaps moved by his officers’ urgency, an involuntary sigh escaped Ho Ga-Myung’s mouth.

“The troops…”

He bit his lip until blood appeared. A word he never wanted to speak, but there was only one order he could give now.

“Withdraw the troops.”

“Co-Commander!”

“Execute it!”

“…Yes.”

A weak reply came from the officers. Hearing it, Ho Ga-Myung squeezed his eyes shut. After a long moment of steadying himself, he opened his eyes and glared at the Heavenly Friends Alliance with eyes full of hatred.

‘Damn it.’

There was no way out now.

It was impossible for the exhausted Ten Thousand People Clan to stand against them. The longer they dragged it out, the greater the losses. If morale remained it might be different, but from the moment they lost sight of the Plum Blossom Sword Ghost, the clan’s spirit had plummeted without end.

If he could gamble everything—sacrifice everyone here—to kill the Plum Blossom Sword Fiend, he might try, but…

‘Mount Hua.’

Those hateful Mount Hua disciples stood around the Plum Blossom Sword Ghost, not moving an inch.

As if vowing not to surrender the Ghost’s neck even if the sky fell.

Seeing that look, an involuntary groan escaped Ho Ga-Myung.

There was no choice. No more.

“Did you not order the troops to withdraw?!”

“Yes! Commander!”

Blood trickled from Ho Ga-Myung’s lips down his chin to the ground.

Woooooooong!

Sweat running down Tang Gunak’s jaw fell to the ground in succession.

His face looked utterly spent, yet his eyes retained a terrifying focus, fixed on Chung Myung.

Chung Myung’s body was momentarily covered in pure white frost, then flushes of crimson flared and receded repeatedly.

Fierce yin qi and fierce yang qi. Tang Pae, who was straining to control the two forces raging within Chung Myung, let out a scream.

“Family Lord! No, not any more!”

Despite hearing the desperation in that scream, Tang Gunak didn’t look away.

“Family Lord! His qi and blood will rupture completely! It can’t continue!”

Tang Pae’s voice grew unbearably urgent. But Tang Gunak only examined Chung Myung’s condition with a stern face.

‘What on earth is he thinking?’

He couldn’t hold on. No matter how Chung Myung was, this was beyond endurance.

Chung Myung was, of course, an astonishing martial artist beyond imagining, but his greatness lay in his sword and spirit, not his body!

But something else was truly astonishing.

Tang Gunak, sweating like rain as he watched Chung Myung, suddenly thrust his hand into his bosom and drew out a white mother-of-pearl vial, popping its cap open.

‘Poison?’

The briny tang that brushed his nose told him instantly it was the deadliest of poisons.

“F-family Lord! Aah! What are you doing!”

Tang Pae screamed as Tang Gunak poured the opened poison vial straight into his own mouth.

Tang Gunak’s skin turned purple in an instant. Yet he didn’t flinch; he reached out and placed his hand against the center of Chung Myung’s chest.

Woooooooong!

His internal qi swelled crazily, and a purple aura rose from his fingertips. Those qis began to surge into Chung Myung’s chest.

Tang Pae’s eyes opened wide as if they’d split.

‘Poison… into the patient?’

What kind of insane act was this?

“Family Lord! The Taoist will die! What are you thinking!”

Tang Gunak glanced up. But his gaze did not fall on the screaming Tang Pae; it fell on Hyun Jong, who watched the whole scene in stony silence.

“It will be fine.”

“There’s no way this is fine!”

“Clashing qi alone doesn’t kill anyone. Death is when the body can no longer contain its internal qi!”

Tang Pae looked at Tang Gunak with trembling eyes. Tang Gunak’s gaze was full of conviction.

“Putting it differently, if you can somehow retain the qi within that body by any means, he will not die.”

At that moment, a change occurred in Chung Myung’s body.

A distinct boundary formed where his heating and freezing had alternated: the top shrouded in a faint purple, the lower-left in red yang qi, the lower-right in white yin energy.

“Ah…”

“If poison, yang qi, and yin qi can counterbalance each other, he can live until that balance collapses.”

Tang Pae’s eyes wavered.

“Then what after that? If the balance collapses?”

“…”

Tang Gunak remained silent, but Tang Pae fully grasped the meaning of that silence.

“Th-this is…”

He had never heard of such extreme treatment. It was less a cure than forced life support—an extreme method that could kill even those who might otherwise live.

“Remove your hand!”

At Tang Gunak’s shout, Meng So, Seol So-Baek, and Han Yi-Myung—who had been frantically pouring internal qi into Chung Myung—collapsed from exhaustion.

Having pushed the poisonous qi fully in, Tang Gunak also removed his hand and stepped back from Chung Myung.

Woooooong.

The remaining qis inside Chung Myung raged. Yet Tang Gunak could see them checking one another and finding a precarious balance.

It was an explosive equilibrium that could collapse at any moment, but with careful attention…

“Damn it!”

Tang Gunak cried out. The poisonous qi he had injected, along with the Ice Palace’s yin qi, was pushing back Meng So’s yang qi.

Compared to his poison and the Ice Palace’s yin qi, Meng So’s yang qi lacked sufficient refinement.

Tang Gunak suddenly raised his head and shouted.

“Any spiritual pills! Does anyone have a pill containing yang qi?”

At those words, everyone glanced at each other with tense eyes, but no one stepped forward.

“We have Heavenly Violet Pill!”

“The Heavenly Violet Pill won’t do! It’s not a yang-dominant spiritual pill!”

“Th-then…”

“Oh no.”

Despair crept across Tang Gunak’s face. If things stayed like this, the balance would collapse and Chung Myung’s body would rupture.

“I can still squeeze out yang qi!”

Meng So yelled desperately, but Tang Gunak could say nothing. This wasn’t a matter of quantity of yang qi. It required a purer yang-dominant spiritual pill.

Tang Gunak’s face was stained with helpless despair.

“Lord of the Tang Family!”

At the very instant Meng So and Hyun Jong cried out in desperation…

Whoosh!

Tang Gunak raised his hand and caught something flying toward him. The yang qi he felt at once widened his eyes.

“It’s Southern Island Sect’s Tidal Sun Pill. When it comes to yang qi, Southern Island Sect is second to none. That should save Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“Sect leader Geum?”

Geum Yang-Baek, the Southern Island’s sect leader who had joined them, nodded with a grave expression.

“Sect leader! Why won’t you take it yourself!”

The Southern Island elders who had raced after him gasped out those words.

Tang Gunak’s eyes fell on the long wound that cut across Geum Yang-Baek’s chest, running down past the sternum to the lower abdomen.

“Sect leader…”

A bitter smile touched Geum Yang-Baek’s lips.

“My wound cannot be mended by any spiritual pill.”

“…”

“What are you doing! Tang Family Lord! Our benefactor is in dire straits!”

At that stern rebuke, Tang Gunak nodded and slid the pill bolt in his hand straight into Chung Myung’s mouth.

As the pill dissolved and its qi flowed into the body, Tang Gunak channeled his essence with all his might.

Woooooooong!

Chung Myung’s body was engulfed in scorching heat.

‘Good!’

This amount of yang qi would do! It could restore the broken balance.

And then.

Woooooong.

“It’s done!”

The qis inside Chung Myung finally reached equilibrium. The qis watched one another warily and coiled in place, unable to strike rashly.

“Family Lord! Chung Myung is?”

Tang Gunak scooped Chung Myung up and shouted.

“He’s breathing for now! But for proper treatment we must get him to the Northern lands as soon as possible.”

Seeing Chung Myung in Tang Gunak’s arms, Hyun Jong called out with power in his voice.

“Mount Hua disciples, hear me!”

“Yes! Former Sect Leader!”

“Cut open a path. Spare nothing that stands before you! Show them how merciless Mount Hua’s sword can be!”

“We obey!”

Mount Hua’s murderous swords rose again.

As Mount Hua swordsmen surged forward, Hyun Jong reached out and supported Baek Cheon.

“Lean on me.”

“F-former Sect Leader. I can walk on my own…”

“Baek Cheon.”

“…”

A soft voice from Hyun Jong reached Baek Cheon’s startled ear.

“You’ve suffered so much. Now let’s go home.”

Baek Cheon turned his dazed face. He saw Hyun Jong’s gentle smile. Choking back emotion, Baek Cheon bit his lip and silently nodded.

“…Yes, Sect leader.”

Those carrying the wounded ran toward the path Mount Hua’s blades had cut open.

Covered in coagulated blood, but now heading down a road that was no longer hopeless.

Chapter 1404: That Was Amazingly Splendid. (4)

“Those who block us will die!”

The booming shout, together with the sword held in that hand, clearly revealed their resolve.

Shaaang!

A lightning-like sword qi poured toward the Ten Thousand People Clan members. Faced with that terrifying force, the Ten Thousand People Clan members could not stand their ground and several of them staggered back.

“Uaaah!”

The crimson plum-blossom sword aura crashed down upon the retreating Ten Thousand People Clan members.

“You bastards!”

“Gwak Hwi, you bastard! Hold the line!”

“Don’t get excited! Our purpose is not to defeat them!”

Gwak Hwi, who had been about to charge the retreating enemies, bit his lip hard.

Mount Hua’s disciples were furious beyond measure, yet at the same time coldly composed. They knew exactly why they had to wield their swords now.

Each time the crimson sword qi burst forth, enemies collapsed in droves. The crimson qi exhaled by Mount Hua, at the vanguard, became a clear beacon to the other sects.

“Follow Mount Hua!”

The Namgung Family and the Five Palaces Beyond the Great Wall, who had been stopping the enemies with all their might, also began to rush forward following Mount Hua.

“Y-you bastards!”

When those who had been fiercely attacking tried to pull back, fury filled the eyes of the Ten Thousand People Clan. Even if they had been ordered to retreat, to let the enemies go after being toyed with in every way was a humiliation.

But their boiling rage and ferocity cooled quickly in the face of the poisonous weapons that flew through the air like a shroud.

“Cover them!”

Ssshhh!

Poisoned needles rained down like a deluge.

“Ugh…!”

“Th-this…”

The needles carried such extreme toxin that even a graze would cause flesh to rot. The only fools willing to leap into that rain of poisoned needles would be lunatics who did not value their lives.

“Burst it open!”

Pfooom! Pfooooom!

As the rain of poisoned needles pushed the enemies back, black poisonous mist billowed up one after another into the gaps that formed.

“R-retreat!”

“Hold your breath! Damn it, fall back!”

The Evil Sect were originally accustomed to poisons. They, too, would not hesitate to coat their weapons with venom or throw poisoned projectiles to achieve their aims.

But what now cloaked the sight was a virulent poison far more deadly than anything they had used before.

The Sichuan Tang Family, the masters of poison across the land. There would not be a single madman in the world who would want to personally test the might of their venom.

A wall of poisoned needles and venomous mist had formed. This wall, though not a solid wall, completely blocked the Ten Thousand People Clan members from advancing.

“Use this gap to go! We’ll cover the rear!”

Those filled with renewed courage ran past the Tang Family. Even in that moment, the Tang Family elders stood like iron towers, gripping their hidden weapons tightly and staring at the Ten Thousand People Clan members beyond the poisonous mist.

“After all our allies have withdrawn, we will fall back too! Hold your positions to the end!”

“Yes.”

The Tang Family elder who gave the order wore a strange smile.

‘Who would have thought we’d volunteer for such a role.’

A family that cared for nothing beyond its own security, cold and merciless as they come—that was the world’s assessment of the Sichuan Tang Family. And the Sichuan Tang Family had no desire to step forward and deny that judgment.

And yet now they were volunteering to cover the rear for the other sects, without anyone ordering them to.

‘The Tang Family has really changed a lot.’

The currents that rose when Mount Hua’s Sword Saint came to Sichuan and drove out the elders—the changes had even begun to alter the Tang Family’s identity.

“…It’s not such a bad feeling.”

The Tang Family elder who smiled gripped the dagger in his hand tightly.

“C-commander! They’re escaping!”

“They are currently withdrawing!”

The obvious reports kept pouring in like blows to the ear. Ho Ga-Myung barely suppressed the urge to announce, ‘I can see too.’

They were distancing themselves, like waves that had surged in, toyed with the shore, and then departed.

Ho Ga-Myung’s gaze was fixed on one spot beyond the dense poisonous mist.

A place encircled like an iron net by Mount Hua’s black-clad sword guardians. It was where the Plum Blossom Sword Ghost, whom he had tried so hard to strangle, stood.

“…There’s no choice.”

Ho Ga-Myung slowly closed his eyes.

He had done everything he could. He had poured in all his power. Yet at this moment that Plum Blossom Sword Ghost was once again slipping from his grasp.

With his own skill, that uncanny judgment, and even incomprehensible fortune all combined.

Ho Ga-Myung could only admit the truth: with his abilities, he could not capture that person.

“Pull the wounded out. If left, they’ll be poisoned to death.”

“C-commander!”

“We will reorganize our ranks and chase again.”

At those words, the lieutenants lowered their heads.

In that instant, Ho Ga-Myung acknowledged that all their plans had gone up in smoke. It meant that all the sacrifices made to capture a single Plum Blossom Sword Ghost had been for naught.

How much had they sacrificed? From the Southern Island to here, the dead numbered in the hundreds. And what had they gained? At most they had captured maybe half of the Southern Island Sect’s disciples.

They had experienced defeat countless times. But in the entire history of the Ten Thousand People Clan, there had been no loss as bitter as this.

“Didn’t I tell you to reorganize the ranks?”

When Ho Ga-Myung issued the cold command again, the lieutenants nodded. Their movement was feeble, but it had to be. Anyone who planned and executed strategy would know how much they had lost from this.

The lost manpower was truly nothing compared to what they had actually lost.

The truly horrible fact was that they had, with their own hands, built for that Plum Blossom Sword Ghost an invincible reputation.

The feat of traversing the south, rescuing the Southern Island, evading pursuit by the Ten Thousand People Clan, and returning to the north.

The countless acts the Plum Blossom Sword Ghost had displayed in that process would now become legend.

And as the owner of that legend, the Plum Blossom Sword Ghost would face the Four Sects Alliance again. Wherever the Plum Blossom Sword Ghost existed, the righteous sects’ morale would soar to the heavens, and the Ten Thousand People Clan forced to face him would be engulfed in fear.

Those who discussed strategy had to understand: an unassailable reputation was terrifying.

They had, with their own hands, perfected the Ten Thousand People Clan’s most perfect natural enemy.

Ho Ga-Myung cast a quick look at his lieutenants. Their expressions alone revealed what they were thinking.

Fools, Ho Ga-Myung clicked his tongue inwardly and said briefly.

“Do you think it’s all over?”

“…”

“Of course, with our current strength, even if we continued pursuit we couldn’t stop them. That’s certain.”

As that cold assessment was made, the lieutenants’ faces darkened further. But Ho Ga-Myung paid it no mind and continued.

“But none of that matters. Have you forgotten that the Alliance lord is heading to the very place they are going?”

At those words, the lieutenants snapped their heads up.

“They are certainly formidable. But don’t forget: from now on the one they must face is none other than Hegemony Lord Jang Ilso. We are little more than a piece in the Alliance lord’s hand. And pieces do not know defeat!”

It might not have been the most appropriate speech to raise morale, but a vitality that had not been there moments before bloomed in the lieutenants’ eyes.

He was right.

They may have been defeated. But they were the Ten Thousand People Clan and not the Ten Thousand People Clan at the same time; and they were the Four Sects Alliance and not the Four Sects Alliance. Without Jang Ilso, the Ten Thousand People Clan could not be the Ten Thousand People Clan, and without Jang Ilso, the Four Sects Alliance could not be the Four Sects Alliance.

“Move. The Alliance lord must have foreseen all of this. Anyone who clumsily sabotages His plan with self-blame will be torn to pieces by my hand!”

“Yes, commander!”

The lieutenants moved like lightning to reorganize the half-collapsed ranks. Watching that scene in silence, Ho Ga-Myung turned his head and saw the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s forces receding in the distance.

It was a failure without excuse, a perfect defeat that did not even raise anger.

In such a situation, a rather odd thought passed through Ho Ga-Myung’s mind.

‘The Alliance lord…’

How could he have predicted it?

Did he think he would capture the Plum Blossom Sword Ghost? Or did he foresee eventual failure?

Ho Ga-Myung shook his head. There was no need to ponder. He only had to faithfully perform his role.

“Hurry. Reorganize the ranks at once and pursue them!”

He was nothing but a mere piece.

There was no need to guess the mind of the one who played the game. No matter how much he pondered, it would be impossible to fully understand Jang Ilso’s will.

Ho Ga-Myung simply watched with a chill in his eyes as the Tang Family made their final withdrawal.

❀ ❀ ❀

“A, aaah! It hurts!”

“Don’t be such a crybaby, you fool!”

Un Geom struck Jo Gul’s back with his scabbard as Jo Gul screamed on his back. Jo Gul let out a shriek.

“I was going to die, I tell you?”

“Ugh.”

Un Geom shook his head vigorously. Until they had been rescued, tears kept welling up and it was hard to hold back, but now that they were saved his chest seethed with rage.

“But how on earth did you know to come?”

“Huh?”

“You were in the north, weren’t you?”

To Jo Gul’s question, the other members of the Five Swords nodded as if they were curious too. Thanks to that they were alive, but they had never imagined in their dreams that the Heavenly Friends Alliance would come to help.

Crossing the Yangtze river was a matter of resolve, but finding them beyond that river—that was another story.

“We were lucky.”

“Yes?”

Un Geom shook his head.

“The Alliance leader decided to cross, yes, but honestly we were at a loss as to what to do. Then that guy suddenly ran like a madman.”

“That guy?”

“That one.”

Un Geom gestured with his chin toward the rear. He pointed to Chung Myung cradled by Tang Gunak. More precisely, to Baek Ah clinging to Chung Myung’s neck.

“Ah, Baek Ah! Baek Ah! Did that thing guide us to you?”

“No.”

“…Yes? Then?”

“We found Southern Island at the place he led us to.”

“…”

“Spirit creature, spirit creature, and yet it’s impressive. It remembered the scent of those we briefly met. The ones we encountered were the Southern Island sect, waiting at the mouth of the Yangtze river for the right moment to cross. When they saw us they led us here without hesitation.”

“Ah, no, how?”

Answering Jo Gul was none other than Gwak Hwan-So running beside him.

“What’s strange about it? It’s a marker we’ve used countless times.”

“…Huh?”

“Didn’t we send Ja-Yang with you? That guy kept leaving marks. We tracked those marks.”

“Ah, no, that man fell behind somewhere in the middle, didn’t he?”

They had been running at full speed and couldn’t even take care of the weaker Lee Ja-Yang.

“That was enough. Once you came within striking distance, that Baek Ah would be able to find you.”

“Ah…”

Jo Gul laughed as if incredulous.

They had not even arranged anything, yet coincidence piled upon coincidence. But on the other hand, one could say necessity layered upon necessity.

If the Heavenly Friends Alliance had not risked their lives to cross the river, if Southern Island had not attached Lee Ja-Yang to them, and if the Southern Island, who had already crossed through hell, had not had the courage to leap back into hell, then they would not be among the living now.

“Such…”

“Why do you look like that?”

“No, no. It’s just…”

Jo Gul quietly looked up at the sky.

“…Just that.”

Everything they had done was not wrong. It was simply… that thought came to him.

Chapter 1405: That Was Amazingly Splendid. (5)

It was an uncharacteristic reaction for Jo Gul, but no one bothered to single that out.

Just from the wounds on their bodies, one couldn’t help but know how harsh a road he and his companions had traveled.

Having gone through such things, even the legendary Jo Gul might have his temperament change…

“No, but what on earth were you thinking crossing the river? Are you sane?”

No, still, Jo Gul is Jo Gul.

“Gul-a… The former Sect Leader…”

“No, I’m not wrong, am I? What if you’d been caught doing that? You’d be dead for sure. You’re not under the illusion you have two lives, are you? Truly irresponsible, you lot. You only survived because you were lucky; if anyone had noticed you on the way here…”

Jo Gul scolded because Chung Myung was quiet. At that moment, Yoon Jong, carried on Hyun Sang’s back, muttered weakly.

“Elder… Could you move just a bit toward that bastard…? I can’t reach him…”

“Do be reasonable at times, Yoon Jong. He looks like he’s about to die even if you leave him.”

“…That would be fortunate then…”

Yoon Jong shook his head weakly. But what he really wanted to ask was the same as Jo Gul’s.

“But how on earth did you end up crossing the river?”

“There was nothing to debate. The Sect leader… no, the former Sect Leader insisted with sword in hand that we must cross the river and ranted.”

“The, the Former Sect Leader said that?”

“Yes. He berated us relentlessly.”

Yoon Jong opened his eyes wide and looked around. The other Five Swords also stared at him in surprise.

After exchanging glances, Yoon Jong watched Hyun Jong’s back as he ran while carrying Baek Cheon.

‘Not Elder Hyun Young, but the Former Sect Leader?’

In truth, if it had been Hyun Young, it would have been immediately understandable for them. Hyun Young is blunt by nature, and though he seems cold, when crisis strikes he wouldn’t hesitate to risk his life for his disciples.

But imagining Hyun Jong nagging and driving his subordinates to cross the river was hard. And he even managed to drive members of other sects?

“So you mean the Former Sect Leader’s berating was accepted by everyone from the other sects?”

“Do you think they only accepted it?”

Hyun Sang let out a hollow laugh as if the more he thought about it, the more absurd it seemed.

“The head of the Tang Family and the Namgung elders actually scolded us several times for being late.”

“…”

“Moreover, originally the Ice Palace and the Beast Palace were supposed to remain north of the river, but when we came to our senses and turned, they were already following behind.”

“What?”

“No matter how we reasoned, they wouldn’t accept it. And there was no time to argue in the midst of it…”

For a moment, Yoon Jong felt dizzy. Thanks to Meng So and Han Yi-Myung, Chung Myung’s life had been preserved. But if things had followed the original plan, those two might not have been here at all.

Hyun Sang, as if knowing his thoughts, spoke.

“Isn’t it strangely providential?”

“…It is.”

Hyun Sang watched Meng So and Han Yi-Myung running ahead on alert and said quietly.

“We didn’t ask anyone to risk their lives for those in here. Yet those people chose of their own will to stake their lives.”

“…”

“And seeing them save that bastard Chung Myung here brought up many thoughts.”

Yoon Jong unconsciously gripped Hyun Sang’s shoulder with force. An indescribable resonance stirred within him. Hyun Sang’s low voice spoke softly.

“Sometimes, whenever Chung Myung and you lot get up to something, I used to think this: you could live comfortably without all that reckless behavior and rampaging—what worry drives the kids to go that far?”

Hyun Sang’s gaze moved to Chung Myung, who was being carried by Tang Gunak.

“But this time I feel like I saw the answer. Perhaps that brat wanted to see a scene like this happen.”

Yoon Jong, like Hyun Sang, stared at Chung Myung’s back.

‘He called it the Heavenly Friends Alliance…’

He had assumed the name was given without much thought. But now it seemed that wasn’t entirely the case.

What Chung Myung had truly longed for was a relationship where, in times of crisis, people would drop everything and rush to help—where each would risk their life for the other.

So he acted that way himself, believing that if he did, others would do the same for him.

His reckless disregard for his own safety in favor of others ultimately saved Chung Myung’s life this time.

If those people hadn’t recklessly risked their lives for Chung Myung, he would not be among the living now.

What is one to call this situation?

Only a single word came to Yoon Jong’s mind.

“It’s Dao.”

“…I see.”

Of course. The ways of the world may seem empty, but in the end they proceed rightly. That’s the only way to put it. Of course…

“Luck plays a role.”

“…That is also true.”

One could put it cleanly and clearly like Yu Yiseol would.

Whether by heavenly fortune or otherwise, countless factors aligned so Chung Myung could live—and so could they; that fact doesn’t change.

Perhaps this memory will not fade for as long as they live.

‘Does that brat know?’

How many people risked their lives to save Chung Myung. Of course, unconscious as he is, he probably doesn’t know.

At that moment, Baek Cheon, who was carried on Hyun Jong’s back, turned his head and spoke.

“This is no time for nostalgia.”

“…Huh?”

“The danger isn’t over yet. You know the Ten Thousand People Clan’s main force is moving ahead, don’t you?”

Yoon Jong fell silent.

“If it’s Ho Ga-Myung, he’ll have contacted Jang Ilso. He’ll know exactly where we’re headed. That means, in order to leave the Southern lands, we’ll have to fight the Ten Thousand People Clan.”

Yoon Jong’s face hardened. Then Hyun Jong calmly corrected Baek Cheon’s words.

“Not only the Ten Thousand People Clan, but the Black Ghost Fort is coming as well.”

“…The Black Ghost Fort too?”

“Yes. And the Pirates are sailing the Yangtze river to the north. And of course the Lower District Sect will come, won’t they?”

Baek Cheon’s face turned grave.

“That means…”

Hyun Jong nodded.

“Yes. It’s not just the Ten Thousand People Clan blocking our path. The entire Four Sects Alliance is gathering at that Yangtze point.”

A deathly silence fell around Hyun Jong.

Those who had escaped death after meeting the Heavenly Friends Alliance, and those who had been elated after risking their lives to save comrades—at this moment they realized the reality.

The Four Sects Alliance. No matter how much one resisted awe, that name was overwhelmingly mighty.

At that moment Jo Gul spoke urgently.

“Ah, they haven’t fully gathered yet, have they? The Black Ghost Fort is still coming, and the Pirates are heading north! If we hurry, we might cross the river without facing them all. Right?”

Tang Gunak was the one who nodded at those words.

“The Taoist’s words make sense.”

“Y-Yes, as expected, Family lord.”

“If we hurry, it might be possible.”

“Then we must go quickly, quickly! Senior sasuk! What are you doing! Run faster!”

Jo Gul urged Un Geom on and made a commotion. Hyun Sang glanced at the scene and asked Yoon Jong.

“Yoon Jong. Shall I move aside for you?”

“…Please, I beg you. Even if I die, I want to kill that bastard and die…”

That jest brightened the mood for a moment.

Tang Gunak, who had been watching them with a small smile, suddenly let his eyes cloud over.

He wasn’t wrong, but there was one thing he didn’t bother to say.

No matter how fast they rushed, they couldn’t avoid the Ten Thousand People Clan that had already moved ahead. And Jang Ilso was among that clan’s main force.

Of course, no matter how formidable Jang Ilso might be, and regardless of the unknown strength of the Ten Thousand People Clan’s main force, it would never be easy for the clan alone to stop the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

“…What about the Green Forest King?”

Tang Gunak turned his head and looked for Im So-Byeong.

“Me?”

Brazenly, Im So-Byeong, carried on the Namgung Family elder’s back, glanced at Tang Gunak.

“What do you think?”

Frankly, it was still awkward to discuss openly, but with Chung Myung unconscious, no one else could see the whole board like Im So-Byeong. In terms of cunning, Tang Gunak had to concede several moves to Im So-Byeong.

“Hmm.”

Without needing more questions, Im So-Byeong understood what Tang Gunak sought an opinion on. He frowned, pondered briefly, and then spoke.

“Coldly speaking, the Ten Thousand People Clan won’t be the only force blocking our path. It’s that man himself who is moving the Ten Thousand People Clan.”

Tang Gunak nodded in agreement.

The Sichuan Tang Family is strong. The Namgung Family has lost much of its strength, but still retains power comparable to many sects. The Ice Palace, having sent only a few, might be iffy, but the Beast Palace’s force is formidable enough that most sects would hesitate to show their faces.

And above all, Mount Hua is involved here.

Right now, two people know best the strength Mount Hua possesses. One is Tang Gunak. The other is…

‘It would be Jang Ilso himself.’

He would never underestimate Mount Hua’s power. He might even rate Mount Hua above Tang Gunak. He always has.

“It won’t be easy, then.”

“Yes. Actually, it’s a foolish thing to do.”

Im So-Byeong scratched his temple with a broken fan.

Paradoxically, because the opponent is Jang Ilso, there’s a high probability no full engagement will occur. Jang Ilso hates taking losses. No matter the Four Sects Alliance’s stakes, he wouldn’t want to expend the Ten Thousand People Clan’s full strength just to stop the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

“If we think in the natural order… as long as we don’t arrive later than the Black Ghost Fort, we might cross the Yangtze without shedding a drop of blood.”

“If things go by the book?”

“Yes. If things go by the book…”

For an instant, similar expressions crossed Tang Gunak’s and Im So-Byeong’s faces.

What flashed in both their minds was Jang Ilso’s endlessly arrogant face, mocking the world.

“…It won’t end easily.”

“Probably.”

If things went by the book, no battle would take place.

But precisely because of that, Jang Ilso will definitely stand in their way. He always has.

Jang Ilso is the type to betray the assumed obvious method with an unimaginably unexpected tactic.

“Whatever happens, we must hurry.”

“Yes.”

Resolve settled on Tang Gunak’s and Im So-Byeong’s faces.

Jang Ilso’s moves are unpredictable. No one in the realm can foresee his plans.

Then at least they must reduce the number of plays he can make.

Reaching the Yangtze before the Black Ghost Fort and the Pirates join the Ten Thousand People Clan—that was the greatest task given to the Heavenly Friends Alliance now.

‘In the worst-case scenario…’

Tang Gunak subtly shifted his gaze. He saw everyone running with him. After taking each face in, he looked down at Chung Myung cradled in his arms.

‘The future of the Heavenly Friends Alliance must be preserved at all costs.’

That is the duty of those who survived and the responsibility of adults.

Tang Gunak, having exchanged looks with Hyun Jong, struck the ground with full force and sped up.

‘Only two hours to the Yangtze river!’

Their fate would be decided within those two hours.

Chapter 1406: It’s Time To Wake Up From The Dream. (1)

“…What did you just say?”

Beop Gye swallowed dryly, at a loss under Beop Jeong’s searching gaze. That bewildered expression seemed to convey Beop Jeong’s feelings perfectly.

“T-the Heavenly Friends Alliance has crossed the Yangtze and set foot on the Southern land.”

Beop Jeong fell into heavy silence. Unable to bear the weight, Beop Gye went on to reveal things that hadn’t even been asked.

“According to the news the Beggars’ Union brought us, the Heavenly Friends Alliance that crossed has joined with the Southern Island Sect and has left the river, heading south.”

“They met Southern Island?”

“…Yes.”

“But heading south—what on earth does that mean? Didn’t they go into Southern lands to rescue the Southern Island Sect? They met the Southern Island Sect and are going south instead of returning? Is that report accurate?”

“W-well, judging from the situation…”

Beop Gye nervously wetted his parched lips with saliva.

“It seems a small number, including Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, remained in the rear. The leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance appears to have led the main force to rescue them…”

Beop Jeong’s eyes trembled. His gaze toward the river went blank.

The Pirates’ ship was swiftly cutting through the Yangtze along the waterway. Beop Jeong opened his mouth in stunned disbelief.

“So…they led the entire Heavenly Friends Alliance into the Southern land, where the Four Sects Alliance is gathering, just to rescue a few disciples?”

“…Yes. For that reason…”

A hollow laugh finally escaped Beop Jeong.

“Hyun Jong…this madman has gone and…”

If others heard, it would be a disaster. Even if he were the Abbott of Shaolin and head of the Nine Great Sects, calling the leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance and Mount Hua’s Former Sect leader Hyun Jong a ‘madman’ was unthinkable.

But Beop Jeong had no strength left to keep such formalities. He was sick of Hyun Jong.

“What on earth! What on earth was he thinking!”

Beop Jeong’s scream made Shaolin disciples glance over in alarm. Only after Beop Gye bit his lip and looked at them did the startled disciples slowly create distance from where Beop Jeong stood.

“No matter how precious a few disciples are, how can you stake the fate of the entire Heavenly Friends Alliance on just those few lives? How could such an idiot occupy the position of alliance leader!”

There was not a single understandable aspect to it.

Of course Hyun Jong’s action was itself incomprehensible. What truly maddened Beop Jeong, however, was that the other sects of the Heavenly Friends Alliance accepted that madness.

What kind of place is the Southern lands?

It is the land where Jang Ilso’s Four Sects Alliance sharpens its blades. Moreover, isn’t the Four Sects Alliance currently gathering across the Yangtze? Even a three-year-old would know what would happen once they crossed the river.

And yet the leaders of those famed sects accepted this absurd act? Why?

“…Have they all, all gone mad?”

Beop Jeong kept mulling it over. Had he perhaps missed something? Could there be a reliable way for them to enter the Southern lands and return safely?

But no matter how he thought, there was nothing he could have missed.

The situation was too clear to consider any other possibility. No matter how he turned it over, the Four Sects Alliance was gathering here, and there was not a single ally across the river to help the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

Mount Hua crossing the river could happen; Mount Hua was always a faction that did the unimaginable.

But for the entire Heavenly Friends Alliance to cross the river was unimaginable.

“Abbott.”

Jong Ri-Hyung, Kongtong’s sect leader, looked puzzled as he watched Beop Jeong remain in shock.

“Why are you so enraged?”

“…”

“Of course what the Heavenly Friends Alliance did was utterly foolish. But looking only at the situation, it’s not necessarily all bad, is it?”

“Not bad, you say?”

“Yes, Abbott.”

Jong Ri-Hyung nodded and continued.

“Of course they’ll suffer unforeseen losses, but those losses would fall entirely on the Heavenly Friends Alliance, wouldn’t they? The Four Sects Alliance wouldn’t be able to advance into Henan while the Heavenly Friends Alliance is within their territory. Wouldn’t they be fighting for us in effect?”

At that moment Beop Jeong glared at Jong Ri-Hyung. The chill in his eyes made Jong Ri-Hyung flinch unconsciously.

Beop Jeong spoke.

“Keep speaking. Why did you stop?”

“Pardon?”

“Do you mean that even if the Heavenly Friends Alliance were annihilated, they’d still inflict damage on the Four Sects Alliance, so it wouldn’t be bad for us?”

“Ah, no, that’s not exactly what I meant…”

Jong Ri-Hyung averted his eyes awkwardly from Beop Jeong.

It was true he harbored that thought inwardly, but as a sect leader of the Justice faction who favored consultation, he felt ashamed at weighing pros and cons over the deaths of others in the same faction.

Beop Jeong, glaring at Jong Ri-Hyung, opened his mouth.

“Sect leader, if the Four Sects Alliance and the Heavenly Friends Alliance clash head-on, what outcome do you foresee?”

“Well…”

Jong Ri-Hyung thought briefly then spoke.

“It’s true the Heavenly Friends Alliance has grown extremely powerful, but I think it’s still difficult for them to face the Four Sects Alliance. In the end, the Four Sects Alliance would win. Of course they’d suffer huge losses too…”

“Losses?”

Beop Jeong retorted in a cold tone.

“A-am I wrong?”

“What, exactly, are you looking at?”

“Pardon?”

“What does the Heavenly Friends Alliance even have? Even the Tang Family numbers are only half that of the great sects, and Namgung has lost half its strength.”

“…”

“They say the Beast Palace from the Nanman came to support, but their martial power was never on par with the great Central Plains sects, and only a few from the Ice Palace joined. The Green Forest? Those ragtag types—would they be of any help in this situation?”

“W-well…”

“Places with inflated reputations! The power the Heavenly Friends Alliance can use now doesn’t even equal half their reputation. Do you not understand what it truly means to rush into the enemy ground where that Hegemony Lord has camped under such circumstances?”

Beop Jeong’s eyes turned bloodshot.

“Mount Hua? What could that small number possibly do! The larger the war becomes, the more they’ll be nothing but a handful of sand!”

Jong Ri-Hyung lowered his head with an uneasy look.

‘No, really…’

Of course Beop Jeong was right. It was a point he hadn’t fully considered. Though the sects within the Heavenly Friends Alliance are all notable, only three can truly exert power on their own: Mount Hua, the Tang Family, and the Beast Palace.

They cannot be called weak, but could they alone handle a massive alliance like the Four Sects Alliance? It was nearly impossible.

The moment the three elements, lack of strength, enemy territory, and the Hegemony Lord, combined, the outcome was practically decided.

But that fact alone wasn’t the reason Beop Jeong was furious, was it?

Was the defeat of the Heavenly Friends Alliance by the Four Sects Alliance something to mourn anew?

Jong Ri-Hyung furtively gauged Beop Jeong and then spoke.

“I…didn’t realize, Abbott, that you cared for them so much.”

A sigh escaped Beop Jeong’s mouth at that. The frustrating man still didn’t understand the situation.

“You really don’t get it, do you?”

“…Sorry?”

“What if the Hegemony Lord orchestrated this entire situation? After swallowing the Heavenly Friends Alliance, where would their next spearhead aim?”

Jong Ri-Hyung’s eyes widened.

“T-that would…”

“Do you think they’d endure losses to topple the Heavenly Friends Alliance and then wait while we obviously send pleas for aid and prepare to face the Four Sects Alliance?”

Of course not. Especially not Jang Ilso.

They would not concern themselves with the Four Sects Alliance’s condition; they would strike at the unassembled Nine Great Sects.

“…Henan.”

“Do you understand now?”

Beop Jeong shouted suddenly.

“Those we’ve sent word to are not responding! There is no support coming here! That means if they bring down the Heavenly Friends Alliance this quickly, it will be us who must stop their crossing over the river—us!”

Chills ran down Jong Ri-Hyung’s spine. His gaze turned to the Yangtze.

Who would the Pirates’ ships riding the river carry? Obviously the forces of the Four Sects Alliance, their morale soaring after toppling the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

Even if we follow those ships, can this number of people here stop that ant-like army crossing the river?

‘Could we stop them…’

Of course, if the Four Sects Alliance has been exhausted fighting the Heavenly Friends Alliance, we might somehow hold them off. But if that happens, neither Shaolin nor the coalition will be able to maintain their names.

Then the factions that didn’t come will take the now unguarded Central Plains for themselves.

As the price for offering no help and simply standing by.

“T-that…”

Jong Ri-Hyung’s face finally turned sallow. Beop Jeong cried out in an agitated voice.

“The only way to defeat a stronger force is to pick them off one by one! And coincidentally, the Justice faction is split into three: the Heavenly Friends Alliance, us, and the bystanders!”

“…”

“And that damned Heavenly Friends Alliance has given the Four Sects Alliance the opening to pick us off! Worse, they’ve shoved us into isolation as well! Don’t you see!?”

Jong Ri-Hyung’s mouth opened unconsciously. Only now did he realize how perilously close to disaster they stood.

“This is why…this is why we shouldn’t have many heads. This is why Mount Hua should never be the thinking head…”

Beop Jeong’s voice trembled.

If they and the Heavenly Friends Alliance could have held the far bank firmly, the Four Sects Alliance wouldn’t dare cross the river. Then they could have followed later without issue.

But that insane move by the Heavenly Friends Alliance distorted and crushed the entire situation.

“Abbott, then what should we do?”

When Jong Ri-Hyung asked, Beop Jeong bit his lip. As anger and resentment ebbed, a frighteningly cold reason returned.

Now there was only one factor that would decide the situation.

‘Which side is it?’

How would Jang Ilso view that absurd act the Heavenly Friends Alliance committed?

Was their action an unexpected embarrassment even to Jang Ilso?

Or did he foresee this whole situation?

If the former, there is hope. The power of the Heavenly Friends Alliance wouldn’t be easily dismissed even by the Four Sects Alliance.

But what if the latter? What if Jang Ilso predicted their action? No, what if this movement was all part of Jang Ilso’s plan to create this situation?

‘It’s all over.’

Then the Hegemony Lord would certainly have prepared another move, one that could topple the Heavenly Friends Alliance without suffering losses.

The vision of the burning Yangtze formed before Beop Jeong’s eyes. Fierce flames leapt across the river into the Northern lands, consuming the world in red.

“Send word to the Beggars’ Union and have them reassess the enemy positions! We must know what those bastards are planning!”

“I-it’s difficult! They may not be able to move either…”

“Don’t you realize this isn’t a situation to be sparing with lives!?”

In the end, Beop Jeong bit his lip so hard it bled.

‘We must stop them. By any means necessary.’

His burning gaze turned beyond the river—to the land where the fate of the Central Plains would be decided.

Chapter 1407: It’s Time To Wake Up From The Dream. (2)

Long eyelashes trembled subtly.

After a moment the trembling subsided, and the eyes that had been tightly shut slowly opened.

Those strange eyes, seeming either clouded or refusing to accept anything, were intoxicating just to look at.

They were gazing upward.

The ceiling of the lavish carriage, lavishly adorned with rare precious treasures that most people would never see in a lifetime, came into view.

The silk draped across the ceiling like waves was of the finest grade even among the best celestial silks, a rarity that made gold seem pale.

The silk was decorated with carved precious jades, and the cat’s-eye gems set between them were so valuable that a single one could buy a vast estate.

And that was not all.

The low table within reach was carved from the finest sandalwood said to be fit for kings; even the small cup on it was a craftsman’s carving from a single piece of jade.

Within reach, underfoot, and draped over the body—nothing there was without value.

Yet in the man’s eyes, who held and wore all of it, there was not a trace of desire or pride.

To him, the gaze people cast on these endlessly precious things was merely bored and empty.

Do those things truly have that value? Do they hold value equal to their price?

A small sneer curved the man’s red lips.

“Of course not…”

It’s utterly laughable.

Silk is nothing more than a slightly tougher, more presentable cloth. What real difference is there between someone wrapped in silk and someone in rags?

Those precious gems? They’re not much different from stones on the roadside—merely rocks that glitter a bit more.

That sandalwood, supposedly only for emperors and kings—it’s just wood with a different hue.

Would beasts that are not human distinguish gems from stones? Would they care about the difference between straw and silk in their nests? Would they notice the color of the wood as they climb trees?

They would not.

Only humans assign value to these meaningless things and then believe those values they invented.

Wealth, honor, righteousness, faith, bonds, ideals… so many endless, worthless things.

“Heh heh heh.”

A faint laugh escaped.

In the end, value is what people arbitrarily stick onto things. It can be peeled away at any time.

But people forget that they created those values, and they cling to them, even wagering their most precious lives.

The man picked up the wine bottle on the low table and tilted it slowly; amber fine wine flowed into the jade cup.

“Foolish… and stupid.”

They mock the moths that rush blindly toward the flame, while believing their own act of sacrificing themselves for the worthless is noble.

How could he not laugh? The world is a vast comedy filled with tragedy.

Savoring the rich aroma, the man raised the cup. His reflection trembled faintly over the amber wine.

He stared at himself, draped in costly metals, painted in heavy makeup, wrapped in red and white silk—and he laughed.

Jang Ilso mocked everything—but most thoroughly, he mocked himself.

The many who lived outside this carriage chased what each believed to be valuable—but though Jang Ilso knew it was all meaningless, he refused to let go of any of it.

Even if he could not feel the difference between coarse cloth and silk, he wrapped himself in the finest silk; even knowing cheap liquor and expensive wine are the same once swallowed, he forced only the rarest into his mouth.

“Heh heh heh heh.”

Jang Ilso drained the cup at a gulp.

If risking one’s life for the worthless and clinging to the meaningless are conditions of being human, Jang Ilso was the most perfect human of all.

His gaze turned to the small, square window.

They gather, each holding their own ideal.

Someone seeks alliance, someone power, another loyalty, and someone else peace.

All with different aims converge in one place. Their thick, suffocating desires will swirl together.

Jang Ilso’s lips, red as blood, moved.

“It’s about time… to prepare to receive them.”

A whirlpool will form.

On the board he created—Shaolin, the Nine Great sects, Mount Hua, the Heavenly Friends Alliance, and even the Four Sects Alliance.

What will perish in that terrible whirl are not people or lives, but ideals—illusions someone believed worthy to pursue.

To those intoxicated by ideals, the world will taste sweetest—like moths enchanted by the flame, unaware their wings burn.

But now…

“It’s time to wake from the dream.”

A radiant smile spread across Jang Ilso’s lips. Soon the world would be painted with blood.

❀ ❀ ❀

“Hoo… hoo…”

No more sweat flowed. His pallid complexion, devoid of any color, made Geum Yang-Baek’s condition unmistakable.

But his feet did not cease for a moment. He would run until his last breath left him.

Glancing at Geum Yang-Baek’s state, Hyun Jong quietly addressed Tang Gunak.

“Tang Family Lord…”

“It’s impossible.”

But Tang Gunak, as if already anticipating Hyun Jong’s words, shook his head without hesitation before Hyun Jong could finish.

“His dantian is shattered and his innate qi is flowing backward. Even the Tang Family’s medicine cannot save him.”

“…Isn’t the Tang Family the one who even saved that half-dead Chung Myung?”

“Alliance leader. I fully understand your sorrow, but we are neither Hua Tao nor immortals. There are things we can do and things we cannot.”

“…”

“We could save the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint because he could endure treatment. But not Sect leader Geum. A broken cup, no matter how carefully glued, cannot hold water.”

“Hmm.”

Even as he listened, he couldn’t readily understand.

For outwardly Chung Myung’s wounds seemed far worse. Yet Chung Myung could be saved while Geum Yang-Baek had no remedy.

But Hyun Jong did not press further. He was no expert in medicine, and Tang Gunak was one of the foremost—who was he to argue medicine with him?

He glanced toward Tang Soso with a hopeful heart, but Tang Soso only shook her head as well.

As Hyun Jong secretly sighed, Geum Yang-Baek, struggling to keep up, suddenly staggered and spat out a mouthful of blood.

“Sect leader!”

The startled disciples rushed toward Geum Yang-Baek, but he waved them off and opened his eyes wide.

“Keep going! Don’t worry, just run, now!”

The faces of the Southern Island Sect’s disciples drained of color, but they could not disobey the stern Sect leader’s command. Gritting their teeth, they started running again.

At that moment Gwak Hwan-So approached Geum Yang-Baek’s side.

“Sect leader, let me carry you!”

But Geum Yang-Baek stubbornly shook his head and ran on in silence. Anxious, Gwak Hwan-So caught up and grabbed his arm.

“Then let me at least lend you my qi…”

“Don’t do pointless things.”

Geum Yang-Baek sharply rebuked him and freed Gwak Hwan-So’s hand from his arm.

“Sect leader! This is no time for stubbornness! At this rate…”

“I’ll die?”

“…”

“Is that what you wanted to say?”

When Gwak Hwan-So fell silent, Geum Yang-Baek snorted a laugh—not mocking. Despite his firm, cold gestures, his eyes showed warmth toward his disciples and Gwak Hwan-So.

“I too know the value of my life. If being carried would save me, I would have done it already.”

Geum Yang-Baek said, panting; yet his tone remained calm, making Gwak Hwan-So bite his lip.

“But if it won’t help me a bit, don’t waste your strength on me. We haven’t reached the Northern lands yet, have we?”

“Don’t say that! Why would Sect leader die? Your disciples are still alive—so you must live too! You must live to reestablish the Southern Island sect’s foothold in the Northern lands!”

Gwak Hwan-So’s voice trembled with tears, but Geum Yang-Baek only smiled and did not reply.

Gwak Hwan-So knew too how hollow his words were.

“Hwan-so.”

“…Yes, Sect leader.”

“I’m glad you’re here.”

Gwak Hwan-So’s shoulders trembled. Geum Yang-Baek coughed and turned to him; his face was so pale it had a bluish cast.

“If you weren’t here, even in death I wouldn’t have closed my eyes. But… with you here, dying isn’t so frightening. Haha.”

“Sect leader…”

A crooked laugh escaped Geum Yang-Baek’s mouth.

He had come to die. With no way to live, he thought a final sword dance would suffice—his last duty as the Southern Island’s Sect leader.

But they were still alive. Perhaps, crossing that river, they could carry the Southern Island Sect’s name forward.

So why fear death now?

Geum Yang-Baek quietly looked at Gwak Hwan-So. Would it be strange to say he found his attempt to hold back an impending sob reassuring?

‘You’ve changed a lot.’

Gwak Hwan-So no longer exuded recklessness. Though still young to lead a sect, he would not lack in fulfilling his duties—indeed, he might do far better.

And so…

Yet Geum Yang-Baek swallowed the rising blood in his throat.

‘But not yet.’
